Power Of Symbols ^B5du03ZL

From Concepts to

We don't only use symbolic representation, but we're also characterize it.

Twin-sided Disruption 
of Anomaly ^u77kSM1U

Significance of disruption of anomaly not only regulates negative emotions. It also gives some kind of hope because of promise of radical transformation and insight ^kV35aYKT

Like it happened with your "liver scare" when you find out that you're glad that this happened because you started to feel alive and transform you life. So negative event was met with a lot of stress but also with hope and radical transformation that sent you on somewhat upward trajectory ^8EHUJyQq

Feeds goals ^qBBWoqMP

Motivation influence your perception on more fundamental level than goals, so it means your goal is growing on motivational energy ^1Jni9u1r

When you're lustful - interesting studying becomes useless and unrelative porn becomes primary goal, that's how motivation shaped goals, and goals shaped your perception ^oS2oTQdo

Wrong Attitude ^N7nL6Zn1

Conscious can act wrongly as well,
so when you're doing productive
work it can punish you with boredom
or some thoughts to do something else

So you consciously need to see that
and change your attitude - bring more
attention to the task. ^tFgUnzeW

You had such experience, where was wondering to do something else, or lecture was boring - and as soon as you increased the attention to the topic - you lost yourself in the studying.  ^s1YWtini

Honesty is the Key ^0PkmG1EC

You know when you're not honest,
you won't view things as they are,

So confessing is important, because
it makes you honest with yourself, 
and only in honest state of mind
you can truly regret, analyze and
work with healing your sins ^lBlxiGW0

Optimal mastering of 
a game ^EBYFoHY5

Play a game on the edge of Chaos that you can withstand. It's most engaging way to play a game, because you master a game, and getting better with confronting the Chaos, which is meta-skill  ^GRFwpusG

Evolution with
Order & Chaos ^PobXWVV6

Existence of such fundamental categories can be proven by examining systems of the brain.

We have separate regions&systems to deal with chaotic aspect of the world, and separate regions &systems to deal with orderly aspest. ^pztk6zWG

Fixing Anomaly ^QyP50cMe

On losing a trade don't doubt your survival as failed trader, it's the way of depressed. 

Instead focus on what's minimal resolution at which you can approach the anomaly and to decrease it's probability of appearance in the future ^1UvjTg0A

Pathological propagation of failure 
response across nested frames ^JpLjDqqA

Depression ^YO6f4maJ

Hierarchies ^R3Ryq3VE

In depression and\or low hierarchical position - serotonin low as well, and it promotes overcalculation of response to failure 
AND
underestimating own competence and overestimating competence of others ^EhzKYyZ1

Serotonin ^8T4XC22x

Solution? ^Pge0xa4L

Conscious preparation to predictable failures (as in trading) so you won't go on random nihilistic path.

As well as increasing conscious awareness, so it's kinda like idea of integration. Bring more of background process into light of consciousness ^e6p9na24

Find minimal level of resolution
that can fix the problem ^73MRLBeU

Don't drop the whole frame. Figure out if you can fix anomaly in borders of that frame. 

Like in trading. Don't drop out trading idea if you have some failures. Because it results in you constantly switching between ideas without success.

And obviously track those ideas even if they wasn't successful. When you do analysis - make a small "paper" on that research. Did that, got those results, was mildly biased, conclusion is such and such. ^njdFPuWk

Learn to let go and transform ^YWALH3UP

It's the most important thing one should be learning.  ^wMwoKMto

Sense of meaning
as calibration of progress 
through life ^MUJPMBd8

Meaning happens when you're just enough in Chaos. When too much - you're anxious, when too little - you're bored. 

So you figure out when is too much, when is too little - and then you aiming down on the middle. 

And that's the most optimal way because you will be the most productive. ^JtKwKJS9

Get a cake, and eat it too ^SgiSRRHA

For example, in 6 hours of meaningful trading you can progress more than in 12 hours of anxious trading. Obviously. 

Especially when you're considering long-term. In 12 hours scenario you just burn off one day, tilt, and lose everything.  ^WFrCM4D4

But High Aim is the key ^Oi7sqcd1

You can have this meaning in games, or porn etc. And you know it, because you had it. 

So that's why proper aim is the key. You must search for meaning where you moving upward in real life.  ^lWGrfkIG

Meaning everywhere if dosage is right? ^5ULkJeQa

Idea suggest that if meaning is proper combination of chaos and order in context of upward aim - then does it mean it doesn't matter much what action you're doing? Just as long as you transform upward through that "meta story" structure?  ^WWbQlIBp


And maybe you can leverage that. For trading.

Like it's obviously confronting the chaos - the better you become with it - the better is upward path.

Then it's materialistic things.
Then there's emotional control.
Then there's discipline.
Creative generation of trading system.
Ability to search and leverage patterns in complex systems
Like you can lay out in such way many-many things that are oriented upward besides just money.  ^sDtuMCCv

Faithful decisions ^qrLD6cfL

Truth
I will play straight game
(won't lie, cheat, cut corners, shy away) ^UM1zK7ww

Love
Being is worthwhile
and I will sacrifice towards
its betterment ^IMf9GQKj

What if goal is scary (trading) ^2UgbEILX

"The bigger the dragon - the bigger the treasure"

So when you're scared of trading or fail in it - you must remember that  you're going after the mountain of treasure in so many ways (materialistic and wisdom) ^UUguYcDM
